Structural cue: the surah’s wealth/giving vocabulary has pastoral undertones. Mechanism: the miser’s economy promises nourishment but dries up. Abductive move: I infer a material analogy from failed milk to failed wealth. Reading change: “كَذَّبَ” can carry the surprise of provision that does not endure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_70a17296700647b285d9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:9:1\",\"root_001290\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Besleyici sütün beklenenden önce kesilmesi görüntüsü, cimri ekonominin vaat ettiği geçimin tükenmesini düşündürür; bu pastoral benzetme spekülatiftir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000323/B002","root_001290/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7277748d3454c63069e9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:9:1\",\"root_001290\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:9:2\",\"root_000323\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"En güzeli yalanlamak, verme ve yarar sağlama karşıtlığı içinde, iyiliğin başkasına fayda vererek gerçekleştiği bir düzeni reddetmektir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_99e1998ce54eba19c866","text":"2. **A charge that fails in action.** `ك ذ ب:B004` preserves the image of a charge proving false when it is not carried through, while the governing frame of 92:4 is purposeful endeavor and the outcome in 92:10 is facilitated hardship. That sequence lets behavioral refusal activate a branch beyond verbal falsification. The military-motion analogy is supplied by the branch, but mapping it onto the moral sequence is an explicit abductive step. `كَذَّبَ` consequently reads as failure to carry a course through, not only a proposition uttered against the good.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c1486f7e8de1fdc5143a","text":"2. **Failure to carry through the charge.** Evidence: `ك ذ ب` B004 (a charge/attack proving false by failing), activated by `ص د ق` B004 in 92:6 and the facilitation/falling sequence. Structural cue: صدق/كذب are opposed as fulfillment/failure. Mechanism: the ungenerous path makes a claim of strength but fails in action. Abductive move: I infer that the “lie” may be performative: the life does not carry its professed sufficiency through. Reading change: denial becomes a failed charge, not just a spoken falsehood.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c4c2ed6b5e6517955c66","text":"1. **Repudiation of a beneficent order.** `كَذَّبَ` anchors `ك ذ ب:B002` (declaring something false), and `ٱلْحُسْنَىٰ` anchors `ح س ن:B001` while activating `ح س ن:B002` (beneficent action toward another). The mirrored object in 92:6, giving in 92:5, and withholding in 92:8 make the denied “good” socially operative rather than merely aesthetic. The packet supplies this reversal; the inferred move is that the opposed conduct reveals the practical content of the declaration. Denial thus reads as rejection of an order in which good is made real through benefit and release.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cbfe4490bed75a52187a","text":"92:10–11 made `ك ذ ب:B004` newly visible: denial is followed by a formed difficult course and collapse, like a charge not carried through.
